# flight-sim.html の 5/8 部分（元の 11033〜13096 行目、データは省略）
# 全体の説明は 00_overview.md

  let pick = near.filter(x => x.d < 90000).slice(0, 6);
  if (pick.length < 3) pick = near.slice(0, 4);
  const route = [];
  let cur = [ap.lat, ap.lon];
  while (pick.length) {
    pick.sort((a, b) => geoDist(cur[0], cur[1], a.l[1], a.l[2]) - geoDist(cur[0], cur[1], b.l[1], b.l[2]));
    const nx = pick.shift(); route.push(nx.l); cur = [nx.l[1], nx.l[2]];
  }
  const pts = [];
  let prev = geoToWorld(ap.lat, ap.lon);
  for (const l of route) {
    const [x, z] = geoToWorld(l[1], l[2]);
    const g = Math.max(jwHeightGeo(l[1], l[2]), 0);
    // stand the ring a little before the landmark (on the side we come from) so it is in view
    const dx = x - prev[0], dz = z - prev[1], dl = Math.hypot(dx, dz) || 1;
    const back = l[3] < 0 ? 0 : l[3] > 60 ? 450 : 150;
    const px = x - dx / dl * back, pz = z - dz / dl * back;
    let top = 0;
    for (let k = 0; k < 9; k++) { const a = k / 9 * Math.PI * 2, rr = k ? 300 : 0; top = Math.max(top, jwHeight(px + Math.cos(a) * rr, pz + Math.sin(a) * rr)); }
    const y = l[3] < 0 ? g + 350 : Math.max(top + 180, g + Math.max(l[3], 0) * 0.6 + 120, 250);
    pts.push({ p: [px, y, pz], n: vnorm([dx, 0, dz]), l });
    prev = [x, z];
  }
  course.rings = pts.map(q => ({ p: q.p, n: q.n }));
  course.tour = pts.map(q => q.l);
  const c = geoToWorld(ap.lat, ap.lon); course.centre = [c[0], 0, c[1]]; course.centreGeo = [ap.lat, ap.lon];
}
function tourPassed(i) {
  const l = course.tour && course.tour[i];
  if (!l) return;
  toast(`★ ${l[0]}：${l[4]}`, 7500);
}

// =====================================================================
//  FLIGHT SCHOOL: a structured training course from ground school to a
//  mock flight test. Each flight lesson is a list of steps with targets
//  and tolerances; the instructor watches the aircraft, calls out
//  corrections and grades every item for the debrief.
// =====================================================================
const FT = 3.28084, KTS = 1.94384;
const w180 = a => ((a % 360) + 540) % 360 - 180;
const SCHOOL = { active: null, analog: false, explain: true, wind: null, gust: 1, hood: false, fail: {}, nav: null, obs: 0, prog: null };
function schoolLoad() {
  if (SCHOOL.prog) return SCHOOL.prog;
  let p = null;
  try { p = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('sorajima-school') || 'null'); } catch (e) { p = null; }
  SCHOOL.prog = Object.assign({ lessons: {}, time: 0, landings: 0, airport: 'RJFM', explain: true }, p || {});
  SCHOOL.explain = SCHOOL.prog.explain !== false;
  return SCHOOL.prog;
}
function schoolSave() { try { localStorage.setItem('sorajima-school', JSON.stringify(SCHOOL.prog)); } catch (e) { /* storage unavailable */ } }

const QUIZ_TITLE = { g1: '飛行の原理', g2: '計器のしくみ', g3: '気象と METAR', g4: '無線と管制用語', g5: '航法の基礎', g6: '緊急時の操作', g7: '人間の能力と安全', g8: 'ヒバリの性能（V スピード）', g9: '性能と重量・重心', g10: '航空法規', g11: '機体のシステム', g12: '飛行操作の要領' };

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
//  syllabus
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
//  attitude + power guides. Real instructors give the target (airspeed,
//  altitude, rate) plus an approximate starting attitude and power, then
//  teach the student to correct by what the instruments show. The numbers
//  here come from the flight model's own equations for the current
//  aircraft, so they stay true when the physics changes.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
function steadyState(kt, fpm, o = {}) {
  const P = AC, A = ACFT, atm = atmos((o.altFt ?? 3000) / 3.28084), W = P.mass * 9.81;
  const flapF = A.wing.flap ? FLAP_DEG[o.flaps || 0] / 30 : 0, gear = A.gear.fixed ? 1 : (o.gear ? 1 : 0);
  const V = kt / 1.94384 / Math.sqrt(atm.sigma), q = 0.5 * atm.rho * V * V, n = 1 / Math.cos((o.bank || 0) * DEG);
  const cl0 = P.CL0 + P.flapCL * flapF;
  const pw = Math.max(0, 1.132 * atm.sigma - 0.132);
  const thrust = thr => {
    if (thr === 'dead') return -0.026 * q * P.S * (P.mass / 1050) ** 0.3;
    if (P.engine.type === 'jet') return P.engine.T * (0.06 + 0.94 * Math.min(thr / 0.95, 1) ** 2) * Math.pow(atm.sigma, 0.7);
    const eta = Math.pow(Math.min(1, V / P.prop.Vd), 0.35);
    return (0.04 + 0.96 * thr) * pw * Math.min(P.engine.T0 * (1 - 0.25 * Math.min(V / P.prop.Vd, 1)), eta * P.engine.P / V) - (1 - thr) * 2.5 * V * P.mass / 1000;
  };
  const forces = g => { const CL = n * W * Math.cos(g) / (q * P.S), a = (CL - cl0) / P.CLa, CD = P.CD0 + (0.02 * flapF + 0.05 * flapF * flapF) * (P.flapCD ?? 1) + (P.CDgear || 0) * gear + P.K * CL * CL; return { a, D: CD * q * P.S, CL }; };
  let g, thr = null;
  if (o.pwr === 'full' || o.pwr === 'idle' || o.pwr === 'dead') {
    const T = thrust(o.pwr === 'full' ? 1 : o.pwr === 'idle' ? 0 : 'dead');
    g = 0; for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) { const f = forces(g); g = Math.asin(clamp((T - f.D) / W, -0.9, 0.9)); }
    thr = o.pwr === 'full' ? 1 : 0;
  } else {
    g = Math.asin(clamp(fpm / 196.85 / V, -0.9, 0.9));
    const need = forces(g).D + W * Math.sin(g);
    let lo = 0, hi = 1; for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) { const m = (lo + hi) / 2; if (thrust(m) < need) lo = m; else hi = m; }
    thr = (lo + hi) / 2; if (thrust(1) < need) thr = 1.01;          // 1.01: not reachable
  }
  const f = forces(g), pitch = (g + f.a) / DEG * Math.cos((o.bank || 0) * DEG);
  const dead = o.pwr === 'dead';
  return { thr, pitch, fpm: Math.sin(g) * V * 196.85, aoa: f.a / DEG, rpm: P.engine.type === 'jet' ? null : rpmFor(dead ? 0 : Math.min(thr, 1), V, pw, dead), ok: thr <= 1 };
}
const r5 = x => Math.round(x / 5) * 5, r50 = x => Math.round(x / 50) * 50;
const pitchTxt = p => { const v = Math.round(p); return v === 0 ? 'ほぼ水平（0°）' : `機首${v > 0 ? '上げ' : '下げ'} 約 ${Math.abs(v)}°`; };
const aiRef = p => { const a = Math.abs(p); return a < 1.5 ? '姿勢指示器の小さな飛行機を水平線に合わせる' : `姿勢指示器で小さな飛行機が水平線の${p > 0 ? '上' : '下'}${a < 7 ? `、横線（10°）の半分${a < 4 ? 'より手前' : 'くらい'}` : a < 12 ? '、横線 1 本（10°）くらい' : '、横線 1 本より先'}`; };
function pwrTxt(ss, o) {
  if (o.pwr === 'dead') return 'エンジン停止（プロペラは風で回るだけ）';
  if (o.pwr === 'full') return `スロットル全開${ss.rpm ? `（約 ${r50(ss.rpm).toLocaleString()} rpm）` : ''}`;
  if (o.pwr === 'idle') return `スロットル アイドル${ss.rpm ? `（約 ${r50(ss.rpm).toLocaleString()} rpm）` : ''}`;
  return `スロットル 約 ${r5(ss.thr * 100)}%${ss.rpm ? `（約 ${r50(ss.rpm).toLocaleString()} rpm）` : ''}`;
}
// o: {kt, fpm, pwr:'full'|'idle'|'dead', flaps, bank, how}. `how` picks the correction advice.
function guideText(o, L) {
  const ss = steadyState(o.kt, o.fpm || 0, Object.assign({ altFt: L && L.alt0 }, o));
  if (!ss.ok) return `目安：この機体ではスロットル全開でも ${o.kt} kt・${o.fpm} fpm は出せません。速度を優先してください。`;
  const how = {
    climbFull: '速度が速ければ機首を 1〜2° 上げ、遅ければ下げる。出力は全開のまま（速度は機首で合わせる）',
    level: '高度が 50 ft ずれたら機首を 1〜2° 動かして戻す。速度のずれはスロットル（R/F）で直し、落ち着いたらトリム（T）',
    rate: '昇降率のずれは出力で、速度のずれは機首で直す。目標高度の 10% 手前からレベルオフ',
    glide: '速度が速ければ機首を上げ、遅ければ下げる。出力で直せないので速度は機首だけで保つ',
    turn: '高度が下がり始めたら機首を少し上げる。沈みが止まらなければバンクを少し浅くしてから引く',
    approach: 'PAPI が白 3〜4（高い）ならパワーを少し絞り、赤 3〜4（低い）なら足す。速度のずれは機首で直す',
    slow: '低速では速度は機首、高度はパワー。高度が下がればパワーを足し、速度が落ちれば機首を下げる',
    pattern: 'このままでは低くなりそうならパワーを足し、高くなりそうなら絞る。速度のずれは機首で直す',
    ils: '横の針が下（自分が高い）ならパワーを少し絞り、上（低い）なら足す。縦の針の方へ 2〜5° ずつ針路を直す。速度のずれは機首で',
  }[o.how || 'level'];
  const rate = o.pwr ? `（昇降率 約 ${o.pwr === 'full' ? '+' : ''}${r50(ss.fpm).toLocaleString()} fpm）` : '';
  return `目安：${pwrTxt(ss, o)}・${pitchTxt(ss.pitch)}${rate}${o.bank ? `・バンク ${o.bank}°` : ''}${o.flaps ? `・フラップ ${flapName(o.flaps)}` : ''}。${aiRef(ss.pitch)}。直し方：${how}。`;
}
// target helpers: [value (number or function of lesson state), tolerance]
const T_ALT = (tol = 100) => [L => L.alt0, tol], T_HDG = (tol = 10) => [L => L.hdg0, tol], T_SPD = (kt, tol = 10) => [kt, tol];
function stepSL(name, secs, kt = 100, extra = {}) {
  return Object.assign({ name, g: { kt, how: 'level' }, say: `直線水平飛行：高度 ${'{alt0}'} ft・針路 {hdg0}°・${kt} kt を保つ（${secs} 秒）`, tgt: { alt: T_ALT(), hdg: T_HDG(), spd: T_SPD(kt) }, dur: secs, grade: true }, extra);
}
function stepTurn(dir, bank, name, extra = {}) {
  const side = dir < 0 ? '左' : '右', kt = extra.tgt && extra.tgt.spd ? extra.tgt.spd[0] : 100;
  return [
    Object.assign({ name, g: { kt, bank, how: 'turn' }, say: `${side}へバンク ${bank}° で 360° 旋回。高度を保つ（引き起こしとパワーで沈みを防ぐ）`, tgt: { alt: T_ALT(), abank: [bank, 5] }, gradeWhen: (c, L, st) => Math.abs(st.turned) > 25 && Math.abs(st.turned) < 330,
      until: (c, L, st) => Math.abs(st.turned) >= 345, max: 150, grade: true, onStart: (L, c, st) => { st.dir = dir; },
      check: (c, L, st) => { if (Math.sign(st.turned || dir) !== dir && Math.abs(st.turned) > 20) st.note = '旋回方向が逆です'; } }, extra),
    { name: `${name}：ロールアウト`, g: { kt, how: 'level' }, say: `開始時の針路 {hdg0}° で翼を水平に戻す`, tgt: { hdg: T_HDG(), alt: T_ALT() }, until: (c, L, st) => Math.abs(c.bank) < 5 && st.t > 2, max: 15, grade: true, gradeFinal: true },
  ];
}
const SYLLABUS = [

          result: (L, st) => [{ name: 'アウトバウンド時間', val: `${Math.round(st.t)} 秒`, std: '60 ±15 秒', pass: Math.abs(st.t - 60) <= 15 }] },

      ] },
  ] },
  { id: 's6', title: '第 6 段階　実地試験（模擬）', desc: 'これまでの項目をまとめて審査。すべて合格で修了。', lessons: [
    { id: 'c1', title: '模擬実地試験', goal: '離陸・基本操縦・失速・エンジン故障・着陸を通しで審査', brief: ['試験官の指示に従って飛びます。<b>1 項目でも基準外なら不合格</b>。', '内容：離陸 → 直線水平 → 急旋回（左）→ パワーオフ失速 → 模擬エンジン故障（空港へ滑空・着陸）。', '落ち着いて、各項目の前に目標値を確認しましょう。'],
      std: '各レッスンの基準と同じ', setup: { at: 'runway' },
      steps: [

        { name: '離陸許可の復唱', atc: 'takeoff' },
        { name: '離陸', g: '目安：スロットル全開（回転が約 2,300 rpm まで上がるのを確認）。52 kt で機首上げ 約 8〜10°（姿勢指示器の横線 1 本弱）にしてローテーション。直し方：左に振られたら右ラダー（E）でセンターラインへ', say: 'センターラインを保って離陸、73 kt で上昇', until: c => !c.onGround && c.agl > 3, max: 90, grade: true, tgt: { cl: [0, 8] }, gradeWhen: c => c.onGround && c.kias > 15 },
        { name: '上昇', g: { kt: 73, pwr: 'full', how: 'climbFull' }, say: '73 kt で地上 2,000 ft まで上昇', tgt: { spd: T_SPD(73) }, gradeWhen: c => c.aglR > 200, until: c => c.aglR >= 2000, max: 300, grade: true, onEnd: (L, c) => { L.alt0 = Math.round(c.alt / 100) * 100 + 0; } },
        { name: '水平へ', g: { kt: 100, how: 'level' }, say: '水平飛行に移り 100 kt へ', until: (c, L, st) => st.t > 20, max: 25, onEnd: (L, c) => { L.alt0 = Math.round(c.alt / 50) * 50; L.hdg0 = Math.round(c.hdg); } },

        { name: '失速の準備', g: '目安：スロットル アイドル。高度を保つように、1 秒に 1° くらいのペースで機首を上げ続ける。失速警報 → 振動（バフェット）→ 機首や片翼が落ちる、の順に来る', say: 'フラップ 20°・パワーアイドル。高度を保ちながら失速させる', until: c => c.stall && c.kias < 52, max: 120 },
        { name: '失速からの回復', g: '目安：機首を水平線付近（0〜+5°）まで下げる → 全開 → 翼を水平。60 kt を超えたら上昇姿勢（約 +10°）へ。直し方：失速警報がまた鳴ったら引きすぎ。少し戻す', say: '回復！ 機首を下げる → フルパワー → 翼水平 → 上昇へ', until: (c, L, st) => c.vs > 100 && c.kias > 62 && st.t > 2, max: 40, grade: true, onStart: (L, c, st) => { st.min = c.alt; st.start = c.alt; }, check: (c, L, st) => { st.min = Math.min(st.min, c.alt); },
          result: (L, st) => [{ name: '失速回復の高度損失', val: `${Math.round(st.start - st.min)} ft`, std: '300 ft 以内', pass: st.start - st.min <= 300 }] },
        { name: '模擬エンジン故障', g: { kt: 65, pwr: 'dead', how: 'glide' }, say: 'エンジン故障！ 65 kt で空港へ滑空し、滑走路に着陸', fail: 'engine', until: c => c.onGround && c.gsK < 3, max: 600, grade: true, tgt: { spd: [65, 8] }, gradeWhen: (c, L, st) => st.t > 8 && c.aglR > 300,
          check: (c, L, st) => { if (c.onGround && !st.td) { st.td = true; st.paved = c.paved; st.fpm = -c.vs; } },
          result: (L, st) => [{ name: '着陸', val: st.paved ? '滑走路' : '滑走路外', std: '滑走路', pass: !!st.paved }] },
      ] },
  ] },
];
// landing steps shared by several lessons
function landingSteps(vref, xwind = false, short = false) {
  return [
    { name: 'ファイナル（安定進入）', g: { kt: vref, fpm: -Math.round(vref * 5.3), flaps: short ? 3 : 2, how: 'approach' }, say: `ファイナル：${vref} kt・3°（PAPI 赤 2 白 2）でセンターラインに乗る`, tgt: { spd: T_SPD(vref, 5), cl: [0, 30], gp: [3, 0.9] }, gradeWhen: c => c.aglR < 500 && c.aglR > 60 && c.u < -c.L2, until: c => c.aglR < 40 || c.onGround, max: 300, grade: true },
    { name: short ? '短距離接地' : '接地', g: '目安：滑走路の端を越えたらアイドル。地上 10〜15 ft から滑走路の先を見ながら機首を少しずつ上げ、最後は機首上げ 約 5〜8°。直し方：浮き上がったら引くのを止めて待つ、沈みが速ければ少し強く引く', say: short ? '目標点に接地。すぐにアイドル・ブレーキ（B）' : '滑走路の端を越えたらアイドル、地上 10〜15 ft でフレアして接地', until: c => c.onGround && c.gsK < 3, max: 120,
      check: (c, L, st) => {
        if (c.onGround && !st.td) { st.td = true; st.fpm = -c.vs; st.u = c.u; st.v = c.v; st.paved = c.paved; st.lat = c.latV; st.crab = Math.abs(w180(c.hdg - c.rwyHdg)); st.tdU = c.u; SCHOOL.prog.landings++; }
        if (c.onGround && st.td) st.stopU = c.u;
      },
      result: (L, st) => {
        if (!st.td) return [{ name: '接地', val: 'していない', std: '—', pass: false }];
        const aim = -RWY.len / 2 + 300, du = st.u - aim, rows = [
          { name: '接地の昇降率', val: `${Math.round(st.fpm)} fpm`, std: '300 fpm 以下', pass: st.fpm <= 300 },
          { name: '接地位置', val: `目標点から ${du >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${Math.round(du)} m`, std: short ? '±60 m' : '−100〜+400 m', pass: short ? Math.abs(du) <= 60 : du >= -100 && du <= 400 },
          { name: 'センターライン', val: `${Math.abs(st.v).toFixed(1)} m`, std: '6 m 以内', pass: Math.abs(st.v) <= 6 && st.paved },
        ];
        if (xwind) rows.push({ name: '接地時の横流れ', val: `${Math.abs(st.lat).toFixed(1)} m/s`, std: '1.5 m/s 以下', pass: Math.abs(st.lat) <= 1.5 }, { name: '機首方向', val: `${st.crab.toFixed(0)}°`, std: '±5°', pass: st.crab <= 5 });
        if (short) rows.push({ name: '停止距離', val: `${Math.round((st.stopU || st.tdU) - st.tdU)} m`, std: '450 m 以内', pass: (st.stopU || st.tdU) - st.tdU <= 450 });
        return rows;
      } },
  ];
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
//  lesson runtime
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
function stepGuide(S, L) {
  if (!S || !S.g) return '';
  const g = typeof S.g === 'function' ? S.g(L) : S.g;
  if (typeof g === 'string') return g;
  let t = guideText(g, L);
  if (g.pp) t = t.replace(/。姿勢指示器で[^。]*。/, '。姿勢指示器は故障中：機首の上下は高度計と昇降計、傾きは旋回計で判断。');
  return t;
}
function findLesson(id) { for (const st of SYLLABUS) for (const l of st.lessons) if (l.id === id) return l; return null; }
function lessonOrder() { const out = []; for (const st of SYLLABUS) for (const l of st.lessons) out.push(l.id); return out; }
function schoolCtx(dt) {
  const s = sim, a = attitude(), [u, v] = worldToRwy(s.pos[0], s.pos[2]);
  const S = SCHOOL, prev = S.prevHdg === undefined ? a.hdg : S.prevHdg; S.prevHdg = a.hdg;
  const rate = w180(a.hdg - prev) / Math.max(dt, 1e-3);
  S.rateF = S.rateF === undefined ? rate : S.rateF + (rate - S.rateF) * Math.min(1, dt * 3);
  const aglR = (s.pos[1] + WHEEL_BOTTOM - RWY.elev) * FT;
  const rwyHdg = RWY.hdg, L2 = RWY.len / 2;
  const latV = s.vel[0] * RWY.perp[0] + s.vel[2] * RWY.perp[2];
  const aim = -L2 + 300, gp = u < aim - 50 ? Math.atan2(Math.max(aglR / FT, 0), aim - u) / DEG : 3;
  const nav = navDeviation();
  return { dt, alt: s.pos[1] * FT, agl: s.agl * FT, aglR, kias: s.ias * KTS, hdg: a.hdg, pitch: a.pitch, bank: a.bank, vs: s.vs * 196.85, rate: S.rateF, u, v, L2, rwyHdg,
    gsK: Math.hypot(s.vel[0], s.vel[2]) * KTS, thr: s.thr, flaps: s.flapIdx, onGround: s.onGround, paved: onPaved(s.pos[0], s.pos[2]) === 'rwy', stall: s.stallWarn, g: s.gLoad, latV, gp, cl: v,
    cdi: nav.cdi, loc: nav.loc, gsd: nav.gs, dme: nav.dme, brgTo: nav.brgTo };
}
// value of a target key in the current context (deviation is value - target)
function tgtValue(k, c, L) {
  switch (k) {
    case 'alt': return c.alt; case 'spd': return c.kias; case 'vs': return c.vs; case 'aglR': return c.aglR; case 'gp': return c.gp; case 'cl': return c.cl;
    case 'abank': return Math.abs(c.bank); case 'arate': return Math.abs(c.rate); case 'cdi': return c.cdi; case 'loc': return c.loc; case 'gs': return c.gsd;
    default: return 0;
  }
}
function tgtDev(k, c, L, tv) {
  if (k === 'hdg') return w180(c.hdg - tv);
  if (k === 'rhdg') return w180(c.hdg - c.rwyHdg);
  if (k === 'dwhdg') return w180(c.hdg - (c.rwyHdg + 180));
  return tgtValue(k, c, L) - tv;
}
const TGT_LABEL = { alt: ['高度', 'ft'], hdg: ['針路', '°'], rhdg: ['針路', '°'], dwhdg: ['針路', '°'], spd: ['速度', 'kt'], vs: ['昇降率', 'fpm'], aglR: ['対地高度', 'ft'], abank: ['バンク', '°'], arate: ['旋回率', '°/s'],
  gp: ['進入角', '°'], cl: ['センターライン', 'm'], cdi: ['CDI', 'ドット'], loc: ['LOC', 'ドット'], gs: ['GS', 'ドット'] };
function fillText(s, L) {
  return s.replace(/\{(\w+)\}/g, (m, k) => {
    const v = { alt0: L.alt0, alt1: L.alt0 + 1000, hdg0: L.hdg0, hdgL: (L.hdg0 + 270) % 360, rwyHdg: Math.round(RWY.hdg), dwHdg: Math.round((RWY.hdg + 180) % 360), brgTo: Math.round(SCHOOL.last ? SCHOOL.last.brgTo : 0),
      obs: Math.round(SCHOOL.obs), obsOut: Math.round((SCHOOL.obs + 180) % 360), intHdg: Math.round(L.intHdg || 0), xc1: L.xc1 && L.xc1.name, xc2: L.xc2 && L.xc2.name,
      xcBrg: L.xcBrg, xcDist: L.xcDist, xc2Brg: L.xc2 ? Math.round(geoBearing(...planeGeo(), L.xc2.lat, L.xc2.lon)) : '' }[k];
    if (v === undefined) return m;
    return typeof v === 'number' ? (k.startsWith('alt') ? Math.round(v).toLocaleString() : String(Math.round(v)).padStart(k.includes('dg') || k.includes('Hdg') || k.includes('brg') || k.includes('obs') ? 3 : 1, '0')) : v;
  });
}
function placeAircraft(pos, hdg, kt, gammaDeg, flapIdx, thr) {
  const s = sim, hasFlaps = !!ACFT.wing.flap;
  s.flapIdx = hasFlaps ? flapIdx : 0; s.flapDeg = FLAP_DEG[s.flapIdx];
  const v = kt / KTS, ff = hasFlaps ? s.flapDeg / 30 : 0, tr = trimFor(v, ff, Math.cos(gammaDeg * DEG));
  s.trim = clamp(tr.elev, -TRIM_MAX, TRIM_MAX); s.trimHold = 0;
  s.pos = pos; s.q = qFromHPB(hdg, tr.alpha / DEG + gammaDeg, 0);
  const dir = [Math.sin(hdg * DEG), 0, -Math.cos(hdg * DEG)];
  s.vel = vadd(vscale(dir, v * Math.cos(gammaDeg * DEG)), [0, v * Math.sin(gammaDeg * DEG), 0]);
  s.w = [0, 0, 0]; s.thr = thr; s.n1 = Math.min(thr / 0.95, 1); s.gearDown = true; s.gearPos = 1; s.airTime = 10; s.onGround = false; s.mainContact = false;
  s.rpm = rpmFor(thr, v);
}
async function startLesson(id) {
  const Ls = findLesson(id);
  if (!Ls) return;
  if (Ls.quiz) { openQuiz(id); return; }
  if (Ls.inst) { INST.back = null; openInstTutorial(0); return; }
  if (Ls.read) { openReadQuiz(); return; }
  const P = schoolLoad();
  // training aircraft, airport and settings
  game.acftId = 'trainer'; setAircraft('trainer', false); sim.dead = false;
  game.assist = false; game.ap = false; game.timeScale = 1; game.destIcao = '';
  if (game.depIcao !== P.airport || !JW.active || !JW.dep || JW.dep.icao !== P.airport) { game.depIcao = P.airport; await loadMap('japan'); }
  JW.dest = null;
  $('school').hidden = true; $('menu').hidden = true;
  ENV = Ls.setup.night ? TOD.night : TOD.day;
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-tod]').forEach(o => o.classList.toggle('on', o.dataset.tod === (Ls.setup.night ? 'night' : 'day')));
  const L = { id, def: Ls, step: -1, results: [], t: 0, call: 'JA4' + String(100 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 899)).padStart(3, '0'), apName: apEnglish(JW.dep) };
  SCHOOL.active = L; SCHOOL.fail = {}; SCHOOL.hood = !!Ls.setup.hood; SCHOOL.analog = true; SCHOOL.prevHdg = undefined; SCHOOL.rateF = 0;
  // wind
  const su = Ls.setup;
  if (su.wind) { const from = RWY.hdg + su.wind.rel, sp = su.wind.kt / KTS; SCHOOL.wind = [-Math.sin(from * DEG) * sp, 0, Math.cos(from * DEG) * sp]; SCHOOL.gust = su.wind.gust || 1; L.windStr = `${String(Math.round(((from % 360) + 360) % 360 / 10) * 10).padStart(3, '0')} at ${su.wind.kt}`; }
  else { SCHOOL.wind = null; SCHOOL.gust = 1; L.windStr = `${String(Math.round(RWY.hdg / 10) * 10).padStart(3, '0')} at 6`; }
  // training area altitude: 2,000 ft above the highest ground nearby (at least 3,000 ft)
  const [cx, , cz] = rwyToWorld(0, 0);
  let top = 0; for (let a = 0; a < 16; a++) for (const r of [2000, 5000, 9000]) top = Math.max(top, terrainHeight(cx + Math.cos(a / 16 * 6.283) * r, cz + Math.sin(a / 16 * 6.283) * r));
  L.alt0 = Math.max(3000, Math.ceil((top * FT + 2000) / 500) * 500);
  // spawn
  audio.init();
  spawn(su.at === 'runway' ? 'runway' : 'air');
  game.mode = su.at === 'runway' ? 'runway' : 'air'; game.state = 'flying'; game.t = 0; game.firstTouch = null; game.track = []; game.arrived = false;
  const L2 = RWY.len / 2;
  if (su.at === 'area') {
    const hdg = Math.round(((RWY.hdg + 90) % 360) / 10) * 10, p = rwyToWorld(-1500, -9000, L.alt0 / FT);
    placeAircraft(p, hdg, su.kt || 100, 0, su.flaps || 0, su.thr != null ? su.thr : ACFT.v.cruiseThr);
    L.hdg0 = hdg;
  } else if (su.at === 'downwind') {
    const y = RWY.elev + 1000 / FT, p = rwyToWorld(L2 + 800, -1500, y);
    placeAircraft(p, (RWY.hdg + 180) % 360, 90, 0, 0, 0.55); L.hdg0 = (RWY.hdg + 180) % 360; L.alt0 = y * FT;
  } else if (su.at === 'final') {
    const D = su.km * 1000, aim = -L2 + 300, p = rwyToWorld(aim - D, 0, RWY.elev + Math.tan(3 * DEG) * D - WHEEL_BOTTOM);
    placeAircraft(p, RWY.hdg, su.kt || 68, -3, su.flaps != null ? su.flaps : 2, 0.3); L.hdg0 = RWY.hdg;
  } else if (su.at === 'ils') {
    const D = su.km * 1000, aim = -L2 + 300, intHdg = (RWY.hdg + 30) % 360, p = rwyToWorld(aim - D, -2600, RWY.elev + su.aglFt / FT);
    placeAircraft(p, intHdg, 90, 0, 0, 0.55); L.hdg0 = intHdg; L.intHdg = intHdg; L.alt0 = RWY.elev * FT + su.aglFt;
  } else if (su.at === 'away') {
    const brg = (RWY.hdg + 90 + (su.offset || 0)) % 360, D = su.km * 1000;
    const p = [cx + Math.sin(brg * DEG) * D, RWY.elev + su.aglFt / FT, cz - Math.cos(brg * DEG) * D];
    p[1] = Math.max(p[1], terrainHeight(p[0], p[2]) + 600);
    const hdg = su.toward ? (brg + 180 - (su.offset || 0) * 0.3 + 360) % 360 : brg;
    placeAircraft(p, hdg, 100, 0, 0, ACFT.v.cruiseThr); L.hdg0 = Math.round(hdg); L.alt0 = Math.round(p[1] * FT / 100) * 100;
  }
  if (su.at === 'runway') { sim.flapIdx = 0; sim.flapDeg = 0; }
  // navigation source
  SCHOOL.nav = su.nav === 'VOR' ? { type: 'VOR', lat: JW.dep.lat, lon: JW.dep.lon, id: JW.dep.icao.slice(2) } : { type: 'ILS' };
  if (su.nav === 'VOR') { const g = planeGeo(); SCHOOL.obs = Math.round(geoBearing(g[0], g[1], JW.dep.lat, JW.dep.lon) / 5) * 5 + (su.obsTo ? 0 : 0); if (su.at === 'away' && !su.obsTo) SCHOOL.obs = (SCHOOL.obs + 20) % 360; }
  if (su.xc) setupCrossCountry(L);
  $('hood').hidden = !SCHOOL.hood;
  $('sch-panel').hidden = false; $('touch').hidden = !game.touch;
  cam.mode = 'chase';
  nextStep(schoolCtx(1 / 60));
}
function apEnglish(ap) {
  const EN = { RJFM: 'MIYAZAKI', RJCB: 'OBIHIRO', RJSS: 'SENDAI', RJTT: 'TOKYO', RJBB: 'KANSAI', RJOO: 'OSAKA', RJCC: 'CHITOSE', ROAH: 'NAHA', RJFF: 'FUKUOKA', RJGG: 'CHUBU', RJNA: 'NAGOYA', RJOK: 'KOCHI', RJAF: 'MATSUMOTO', RJTF: 'CHOFU', RJNT: 'TOYAMA', RJFK: 'KAGOSHIMA', RJOM: 'MATSUYAMA', RJSN: 'NIIGATA' };
  return EN[ap.icao] || ap.icao;
}
function setupCrossCountry(L) {
  const g = [JW.dep.lat, JW.dep.lon];
  const cands = JW.airports.filter(a => a !== JW.dep).map(a => ({ a, d: geoDist(g[0], g[1], a.lat, a.lon) })).filter(x => x.d > 35000 && x.d < 110000).sort((p, q) => p.d - q.d);
  const c1 = cands[0] || { a: JW.airports.find(a => a !== JW.dep), d: 60000 };
  const c2 = cands.filter(x => x.a !== c1.a).sort((p, q) => geoDist(c1.a.lat, c1.a.lon, p.a.lat, p.a.lon) - geoDist(c1.a.lat, c1.a.lon, q.a.lat, q.a.lon))[0] || c1;
  L.xc1 = c1.a; L.xc2 = c2.a;
  const p = planeGeo();
  L.xcBrg = Math.round(geoBearing(p[0], p[1], c1.a.lat, c1.a.lon)); L.xcDist = Math.round(geoDist(p[0], p[1], c1.a.lat, c1.a.lon) / 1000);
  L.xcDivertAt = geoDist(p[0], p[1], c1.a.lat, c1.a.lon) * 0.45;
  JW.dest = c1.a;
}
function nextStep(c) {
  const L = SCHOOL.active; if (!L) return;
  L.step++;
  const S = L.def.steps[L.step];
  if (!S) { endLesson(true); return; }
  L.st = { t: 0, turned: 0, stats: {}, holdT: 0 };
  if (S.onStart) S.onStart(L, c, L.st);
  if (S.fail) { SCHOOL.fail[S.fail] = R.time; if (S.fail === 'engine') { sim.dead = true; sim.thr = 0; } }
  if (S.hoodOff) { SCHOOL.hood = false; $('hood').hidden = true; }
  if (S.upset) setUpset(S.upset);
  if (S.name && S.name.includes('ILS')) SCHOOL.nav = { type: 'ILS' };
  if (S.checklist) openChecklist(S.checklist);
  if (S.atc) openAtc(ATC_Q[S.atc](L));
  L.guideText = stepGuide(S, L);
  L.sayText = S.say ? fillText(S.say, L) : S.checklist ? CHECKLISTS[S.checklist].title : S.atc ? '管制の指示に正しく応答する（1〜3 キー）' : '';
  L.hint = '';
  sfxChime();
}
function setUpset(kind) {
  const s = sim, a = attitude();
  if (kind === 'high') { s.q = qFromHPB(a.hdg, 28, -35); s.vel = vscale([Math.sin(a.hdg * DEG), 0.35, -Math.cos(a.hdg * DEG)], 30); s.thr = 0.4; }
  else { s.q = qFromHPB(a.hdg, -22, 50); s.vel = vscale([Math.sin(a.hdg * DEG), -0.35, -Math.cos(a.hdg * DEG)], 62); s.thr = 0.9; }
  s.w = [0, 0, 0];
}
function sfxChime() { try { if (audio.ctx) { const C = audio.ctx, o = C.createOscillator(), g = C.createGain(); o.frequency.value = 880; o.type = 'sine'; o.connect(g); g.connect(C.destination); const t = C.currentTime; g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.06, t); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.35); o.start(t); o.stop(t + 0.4); } } catch (e) { /* no audio */ } }
function schoolUpdate(dt) {
  const L = SCHOOL.active; if (!L || game.state !== 'flying') return;
  const c = schoolCtx(dt); SCHOOL.last = c;
  L.t += dt; SCHOOL.prog.time += dt;
  if (SCHOOL.fail.engine) { sim.thr = 0; sim.dead = true; }
  const S = L.def.steps[L.step]; if (!S) return;
  const st = L.st; st.t += dt;
  st.turned += w180(c.hdg - (st.lastHdg === undefined ? c.hdg : st.lastHdg)); st.lastHdg = c.hdg;
  if (S.checklist || S.atc) { if (st.done) nextStep(c); return; }
  if (S.check) S.check(c, L, st);
  // grading of targets
  let allIn = true;
  if (S.tgt) for (const [k, spec] of Object.entries(S.tgt)) {
    const tv = typeof spec[0] === 'function' ? spec[0](L) : spec[0], tol = spec[1];
    const d = tgtDev(k, c, L, tv), inside = Math.abs(d) <= tol;
    if (!inside) allIn = false;
    const gradeNow = S.grade && (!S.gradeWhen || S.gradeWhen(c, L, st));
    const q = st.stats[k] || (st.stats[k] = { n: 0, inT: 0, max: 0, sum: 0, tol, tv });
    q.tv = tv; q.d = d; q.tol = tol;
    if (gradeNow) { q.n += dt; if (inside) q.inT += dt; q.max = Math.max(q.max, Math.abs(d)); q.sum += Math.abs(d) * dt; }
  }
  st.holdT = allIn ? st.holdT + dt : 0;
  // instructor hints
  L.hintT = (L.hintT || 0) - dt;
  if (L.hintT <= 0) { L.hintT = 1.2; L.hint = instructorHint(S, st, c); }
  let done = false, timeout = false;
  if (S.dur && st.t >= S.dur) done = true;
  if (S.hold && st.holdT >= S.hold) done = true;
  if (S.until && S.until(c, L, st)) done = true;
  if (S.max && st.t >= S.max && !done) { done = true; timeout = true; }
  if (done) finishStep(S, st, c, timeout);
}
function instructorHint(S, st, c) {
  if (!S.tgt) return '';
  let worst = null;
  for (const [k, q] of Object.entries(st.stats)) { const r = Math.abs(q.d) / q.tol; if (r > 0.6 && (!worst || r > worst[1])) worst = [k, r, q.d]; }
  if (!worst) return '良い調子です';
  const [k, , d] = worst, hi = d > 0;
  // which control fixes airspeed depends on the phase, as a real instructor would say it:
  // power is fixed in climbs, glides and constant-rate descents, so the nose sets the speed
  const g = S.g && typeof S.g === 'object' ? S.g : null, pitchForSpeed = !!g && (!!g.pwr || ['rate', 'approach', 'ils', 'pattern', 'slow', 'glide', 'climbFull'].includes(g.how)), slow = g && g.how === 'slow';
  const M = {
    alt: slow ? (hi ? `高度が ${Math.round(d)} ft 高い：パワーを少し絞る` : `高度が ${Math.round(-d)} ft 低い：パワーを足す（低速では高度はパワー）`) : hi ? `高度が ${Math.round(d)} ft 高い：機首を 1〜2° 下げる` : `高度が ${Math.round(-d)} ft 低い：機首を 1〜2° 上げ、速度が落ちるならパワーも`,
    aglR: hi ? '高い：パワーを絞って降下率を増やす' : '低い：パワーを足して降下を浅く',
    spd: pitchForSpeed ? (hi ? `速度が ${Math.round(d)} kt 速い：機首を 1〜2° 上げる${g.pwr ? '（出力はそのまま）' : ''}` : `速度が ${Math.round(-d)} kt 遅い：機首を 1〜2° 下げる${g.pwr ? '（出力はそのまま）' : ''}`)
      : hi ? `速度が ${Math.round(d)} kt 速い：パワーを少し絞る（約 100 rpm で 5 kt）` : `速度が ${Math.round(-d)} kt 遅い：パワーを少し足す（約 100 rpm で 5 kt）`,
    hdg: hi ? `針路が右に ${Math.round(d)}° ずれ：左へ修正` : `針路が左に ${Math.round(-d)}° ずれ：右へ修正`,
    rhdg: hi ? '滑走路の方位より右：左へ' : '滑走路の方位より左：右へ', dwhdg: hi ? '滑走路と平行に：左へ' : '滑走路と平行に：右へ',
    abank: hi ? 'バンクが深い：少し浅く' : 'バンクが浅い：もう少し傾ける', arate: hi ? '旋回が速い：バンクを浅く' : '旋回が遅い：バンクを少し深く',
    vs: hi ? (g && g.fpm < 0 ? '降下が浅い：パワーを少し絞る' : '上昇が速すぎる：パワーを少し絞る') : (g && g.fpm < 0 ? '降下が速すぎる：パワーを少し足す' : '上昇が遅い：パワーを少し足す'), gp: hi ? '進入角が深い（高い）：パワーを足す' : '進入角が浅い（低い）：パワーを足して高度を保つ',
    cl: hi ? 'センターラインの右：左へ（Q ラダー・A）' : 'センターラインの左：右へ（E ラダー・D）',
    cdi: hi ? '針が右：右へ修正' : '針が左：左へ修正', loc: hi ? 'ローカライザーの針が右：右へ' : 'ローカライザーの針が左：左へ', gs: hi ? 'グライドスロープより低い：降下を浅く' : 'グライドスロープより高い：降下を増やす',
  };
  if (k === 'gp') return d > 0 ? '高すぎる（PAPI 白が多い）：パワーを絞る' : '低すぎる（PAPI 赤が多い）：パワーを足す';
  if (k === 'cdi' || k === 'loc') return d > 0 ? '針が右：右へ修正（針の方へ）' : '針が左：左へ修正（針の方へ）';
  if (k === 'gs') return d > 0 ? 'グライドスロープより高い（横の針が下）：降下率を増やす（パワー−）' : 'グライドスロープより低い（横の針が上）：降下を浅く（パワー＋）';
  return M[k] || '';
}
function finishStep(S, st, c, timeout) {
  const L = SCHOOL.active, rows = [];
  if (S.grade && S.tgt && S.gradeFinal) for (const [k, q] of Object.entries(st.stats)) {
    const lab = TGT_LABEL[k] || [k, ''], pass = Math.abs(q.d) <= q.tol;
    rows.push({ name: lab[0] + '（終了時）', val: `${q.d >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${q.d.toFixed(0)} ${lab[1]}`, std: `±${q.tol} ${lab[1]}`, pass, score: pass ? 1 - Math.abs(q.d) / q.tol * 0.3 : 0 });
  }
  else if (S.grade && S.tgt) for (const [k, q] of Object.entries(st.stats)) {
    if (q.n < 0.5) continue;
    const pct = q.inT / q.n, lab = TGT_LABEL[k] || [k, ''];
    const pass = pct >= 0.85 && q.max <= q.tol * 1.6;
    rows.push({ name: lab[0], val: `${Math.round(pct * 100)}% が基準内（最大 ${q.max.toFixed(k === 'cdi' || k === 'loc' || k === 'gs' || k === 'gp' ? 1 : 0)} ${lab[1]}）`, std: `±${q.tol} ${lab[1]}`, pass, score: pct });
  }
  if (S.result) rows.push(...S.result(L, st));
  if (timeout && S.grade) rows.push({ name: '制限時間', val: '時間切れ', std: `${S.max} 秒以内`, pass: false });
  if (st.note) rows.push({ name: '注意', val: st.note, std: '', pass: false });
  if (rows.length) L.results.push({ step: S.name, rows });
  if (S.onEnd) S.onEnd(L, c);
  nextStep(c);
}
function endLesson(complete, reason) {
  const L = SCHOOL.active; if (!L) return;
  SCHOOL.active = null; SCHOOL.fail = {}; SCHOOL.wind = null; SCHOOL.hood = false; sim.dead = false;
  $('hood').hidden = true; $('sch-panel').hidden = true; $('sch-check').hidden = true; $('sch-atc').hidden = true;
  const all = L.results.flatMap(r => r.rows);
  const passed = complete && all.length > 0 && all.every(r => r.pass);
  const scores = all.map(r => r.pass ? (r.score !== undefined ? 0.7 + 0.3 * r.score : 1) : 0.4 * (r.score || 0));
  const avg = scores.length ? scores.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / scores.length : 0;
  const grade = !complete ? '―' : passed ? (avg > 0.97 ? 'S' : avg > 0.92 ? 'A' : 'B') : 'C';
  const P = SCHOOL.prog, rec = P.lessons[L.id] || { tries: 0 };
  rec.tries++; if (passed) rec.pass = true;
  const rank = { S: 4, A: 3, B: 2, C: 1, '―': 0 };
  if (!rec.best || rank[grade] > rank[rec.best]) rec.best = grade;
  P.lessons[L.id] = rec; schoolSave();
  showDebrief(L, passed, grade, reason);
}
function schoolCrash(reason) {
  const L = SCHOOL.active; if (!L) return;
  const c = SCHOOL.last, S = L.def.steps[L.step] || {};
  // a controlled, low-energy touchdown off the runway (or on water) after an engine failure is a survivable forced landing
  const gentle = c && SCHOOL.fail.engine && c.vs > -700 && c.kias < 78 && Math.abs(c.bank) < 20;
  if (gentle) {
    const st = L.st; if (S.check) S.check(c, L, st);
    L.results.push({ step: '不時着', rows: [{ name: '結果', val: `${/海|着水/.test(reason) ? '不時着水' : '不時着'}（機体は損傷・乗員は無事）`, std: '安全に接地', pass: true }] });
    finishStepQuiet(S, st);
    setTimeout(() => endLesson(true), 1800);
    return;
  }
  L.results.push({ step: '事故', rows: [{ name: '結果', val: reason, std: '安全に飛行を終える', pass: false }] });
  setTimeout(() => endLesson(false, reason), 1800);
}
function finishStepQuiet(S, st) {
  const L = SCHOOL.active, save = L.def.steps.length;
  const rows = [];
  if (S.grade && S.tgt) for (const [k, q] of Object.entries(st.stats)) { if (q.n < 0.5) continue; const pct = q.inT / q.n, lab = TGT_LABEL[k] || [k, '']; rows.push({ name: lab[0], val: `${Math.round(pct * 100)}% が基準内（最大 ${q.max.toFixed(0)} ${lab[1]}）`, std: `±${q.tol} ${lab[1]}`, pass: pct >= 0.85 && q.max <= q.tol * 1.6, score: pct }); }
  if (S.result) for (const r of S.result(L, st)) if (!/着陸場所|着陸$/.test(r.name)) rows.push(r);
  if (rows.length) L.results.push({ step: S.name, rows });
  L.step = save;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
//  checklist and ATC dialogs
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
function openChecklist(id) {
  const C = CHECKLISTS[id], box = $('sch-check');
  box.hidden = false; box.textContent = '';
  const h = document.createElement('div'); h.className = 'sc-title'; h.textContent = C.title; box.append(h);
  const ol = document.createElement('ol'); box.append(ol);
  C.items.forEach((it, i) => { const li = document.createElement('li'); li.innerHTML = `<b>${it[0]}</b><span>${it[1]}</span>`; ol.append(li); });
  const f = document.createElement('div'); f.className = 'sc-foot'; f.textContent = 'Enter（またはタップ）で 1 項目ずつ確認'; box.append(f);
  SCHOOL.check = { id, i: 0 };
  markChecklist();
  box.onclick = () => checklistNext();
}
function markChecklist() {
  const lis = $('sch-check').querySelectorAll('li');
  lis.forEach((li, i) => { li.className = i < SCHOOL.check.i ? 'ok' : i === SCHOOL.check.i ? 'cur' : ''; });
}
function checklistNext() {
  const K = SCHOOL.check, L = SCHOOL.active; if (!K || !L) return;
  const it = CHECKLISTS[K.id].items[K.i];
  if (it && it[2] && !it[2]()) { L.hint = `${it[0]}：${it[1]} にしてから確認`; const li = $('sch-check').querySelectorAll('li')[K.i]; li.classList.add('ng'); setTimeout(() => li.classList.remove('ng'), 600); return; }
  K.i++; markChecklist();
  if (K.i >= CHECKLISTS[K.id].items.length) { $('sch-check').hidden = true; SCHOOL.check = null; L.st.done = true; L.results.push({ step: CHECKLISTS[K.id].title, rows: [{ name: 'チェックリスト', val: '完了', std: '全項目', pass: true }] }); }
}
function openAtc(q) {
  const box = $('sch-atc'); box.hidden = false; box.textContent = '';
  const m = document.createElement('div'); m.className = 'atc-msg'; m.textContent = q.msg; box.append(m);
  const p = document.createElement('div'); p.className = 'atc-q'; p.textContent = '正しい応答は？（1〜3 キー）'; box.append(p);
  q.opts.forEach((o, i) => { const b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.textContent = `${i + 1}. ${o}`; b.addEventListener('click', () => answerAtc(i)); box.append(b); });
  SCHOOL.atc = q;
}
function answerAtc(i) {
  const q = SCHOOL.atc, L = SCHOOL.active; if (!q || !L) return;
  const ok = i === q.ok, box = $('sch-atc');
  L.results.push({ step: '無線（復唱）', rows: [{ name: '応答', val: ok ? '正しい' : `誤り（正解：${q.opts[q.ok]}）`, std: '正しく復唱', pass: ok }] });
  box.textContent = ''; const m = document.createElement('div'); m.className = 'atc-msg ' + (ok ? 'ok' : 'ng'); m.textContent = (ok ? '正解。' : '不正解。') + q.why; box.append(m);
  SCHOOL.atc = null;
  setTimeout(() => { box.hidden = true; if (SCHOOL.active === L) L.st.done = true; }, ok ? 2200 : 4500);
}
function schoolKey(e) {
  if (e.code === 'KeyK' && SCHOOL.analog && (game.state === 'flying' || game.state === 'paused')) { SCHOOL.explain = !SCHOOL.explain; schoolLoad().explain = SCHOOL.explain; schoolSave(); toast(SCHOOL.explain ? '計器の読み方を表示' : '計器の読み方を非表示', 1200); return true; }
  if (!SCHOOL.active || game.state !== 'flying') {
    if (e.code === 'KeyI' && (game.state === 'flying' || game.state === 'paused')) { SCHOOL.analog = !SCHOOL.analog; toast(SCHOOL.analog ? 'アナログ計器（6 計器）' : 'グラス計器（PFD）', 1200); return true; }
    if ((e.code === 'BracketLeft' || e.code === 'BracketRight') && game.state === 'flying') { SCHOOL.obs = (SCHOOL.obs + (e.code === 'BracketRight' ? 5 : -5) + 360) % 360; return true; }
    return false;
  }
  if (SCHOOL.check && (e.code === 'Enter' || e.code === 'NumpadEnter')) { checklistNext(); return true; }
  if (SCHOOL.atc && /^Digit[1-3]$/.test(e.code)) { answerAtc(+e.code.slice(5) - 1); return true; }
  if (e.code === 'BracketLeft' || e.code === 'BracketRight') { SCHOOL.obs = (SCHOOL.obs + (e.code === 'BracketRight' ? 5 : -5) * (e.shiftKey ? 0.2 : 1) + 360) % 360; return true; }
  if (e.code === 'KeyI') { SCHOOL.analog = !SCHOOL.analog; return true; }
  if (e.code === 'KeyZ' || e.code === 'KeyO' || e.code === 'KeyL') { toast('訓練中は使えません（自分の操縦で）', 1400); return true; }
  return false;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
//  navigation receiver: VOR (station at the airport) and ILS (active runway)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
function navDeviation() {
  const N = SCHOOL.nav || { type: 'ILS' }, s = sim;
  if (!RWY.dir) return { cdi: 0, loc: 0, gs: 0, dme: 0, brgTo: 0, flag: 'OFF' };
  if (N.type === 'VOR') {
    const g = worldToGeo(s.pos[0], s.pos[2]), r = geoBearing(N.lat, N.lon, g[0], g[1]), b2s = (r + 180) % 360;
    const dist = geoDist(g[0], g[1], N.lat, N.lon), fromDev = w180(r - SCHOOL.obs);
    const to = Math.abs(fromDev) > 90;
    const dev = to ? w180(b2s - SCHOOL.obs) : w180(SCHOOL.obs - r);
    return { cdi: clamp(dev / 2, -5.5, 5.5), loc: 0, gs: 0, dme: dist / 1852, brgTo: b2s, flag: dist < 300 ? 'OFF' : to ? 'TO' : 'FR', type: 'VOR' };
  }
  const [u, v] = worldToRwy(s.pos[0], s.pos[2]), L2 = RWY.len / 2, ant = L2 + 300, aim = -L2 + 300;
  const locDeg = Math.atan2(v, ant - u) / DEG;
  const h = s.pos[1] + WHEEL_BOTTOM - RWY.elev, gsDeg = Math.atan2(h, Math.max(aim - u, 1)) / DEG - 3;
  const inRange = u < ant && Math.abs(locDeg) < 35 && ant - u < 45000;
  return { cdi: clamp(-locDeg / 0.5, -5.5, 5.5), loc: clamp(-locDeg / 0.5, -5.5, 5.5), gs: clamp(gsDeg / 0.35, -3, 3), dme: Math.hypot(u - aim, v) / 1852, brgTo: RWY.hdg, flag: inRange ? 'ILS' : 'OFF', type: 'ILS', gsValid: u < aim - 100 };
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
//  analog "six-pack" instrument panel
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
// every gauge draws from a plain value object, so the same code serves the
// cockpit panel, the instrument tutorial and the reading practice
function gaugeBezel(g, cx, cy, r, label) {
  g.fillStyle = '#15191e'; g.beginPath(); g.arc(cx, cy, r * 1.1, 0, 7); g.fill();
  g.fillStyle = '#0a0c0f'; g.beginPath(); g.arc(cx, cy, r, 0, 7); g.fill();
  if (label) { g.fillStyle = '#9aa4ad'; g.font = `600 ${Math.round(r * 0.145)}px ${UI}`; g.textAlign = 'center'; g.textBaseline = 'middle'; g.fillText(label, cx, cy + r * 0.42); }
}
function needle(g, cx, cy, ang, len, w, col) {
  g.save(); g.translate(cx, cy); g.rotate(ang);
  g.fillStyle = col || '#f2f2f2'; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(-w, 0); g.lineTo(0, -len); g.lineTo(w, 0); g.lineTo(0, len * 0.16); g.closePath(); g.fill();
  g.restore();
  g.fillStyle = '#2a2f36'; g.beginPath(); g.arc(cx, cy, w * 1.6, 0, 7); g.fill();
}
const gTick = (g, cx, cy, ang, r0, len, w) => { g.save(); g.translate(cx, cy); g.rotate(ang); g.fillRect(-w / 2, -r0, w, len); g.restore(); };
const ASI_MAX = () => Math.ceil(ACFT.v.ne * 1.15 / 10) * 10;
const asiAng = k => (-150 + clamp(k, 0, ASI_MAX()) / ASI_MAX() * 300) * DEG;
const vsiAng = v => clamp(v, -2000, 2000) / 2000 * 170 * DEG - Math.PI / 2;
const tachAng = k => (-130 + clamp(k, 0, 3500) / 3500 * 260) * DEG;
const GAUGES = {
  asi(g, cx, cy, r, v) {
    const V = ACFT.v, f = r / 62;
    gaugeBezel(g, cx, cy, r, 'KNOTS');
    const arc = (k0, k1, col, rad, w) => { g.strokeStyle = col; g.lineWidth = w; g.beginPath(); g.arc(cx, cy, rad, asiAng(k0) - Math.PI / 2, asiAng(k1) - Math.PI / 2); g.stroke(); };
    if (V.fe) arc(V.s0, V.fe, '#f4f4f4', r - 8 * f, 5 * f);
    arc(V.s1, V.no, '#2fbf4f', r - 3 * f, 5 * f); arc(V.no, V.ne, '#f2c230', r - 3 * f, 5 * f);
    g.fillStyle = '#e03a3e'; gTick(g, cx, cy, asiAng(V.ne), r - 1, 12 * f, 3 * f);
    g.fillStyle = '#eee'; g.font = `600 ${Math.round(10 * f)}px ${MONO}`; g.textAlign = 'center'; g.textBaseline = 'middle';
    for (let k = 0; k <= ASI_MAX(); k += 10) { const an = asiAng(k); gTick(g, cx, cy, an, r - 2 * f, (k % 20 ? 6 : 10) * f, 1.4 * f);
      if (k % 20 === 0 && k >= 40) g.fillText(k, cx + Math.sin(an) * (r - 20 * f), cy - Math.cos(an) * (r - 20 * f)); }
    needle(g, cx, cy, asiAng(v.kias), r - 6 * f, 3 * f);
  },
  ai(g, cx, cy, r, v) {
    const f = r / 62, ppd = 2.1 * f;
    gaugeBezel(g, cx, cy, r);
    g.save(); g.beginPath(); g.arc(cx, cy, r - 1, 0, 7); g.clip();
    g.translate(cx, cy); g.rotate(-v.bank * DEG);
    const off = clamp(v.pitch, -30, 30) * ppd;
    g.fillStyle = '#2f79c8'; g.fillRect(-r * 2, -r * 2 + off, r * 4, r * 2); g.fillStyle = '#7a4c24'; g.fillRect(-r * 2, off, r * 4, r * 2);
    g.strokeStyle = '#fff'; g.lineWidth = 1.5 * f; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(-r * 2, off); g.lineTo(r * 2, off); g.stroke();
    g.lineWidth = f; for (const p of [-20, -10, 10, 20]) { const y = off - p * ppd, w = (Math.abs(p) === 10 ? 14 : 22) * f; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(-w, y); g.lineTo(w, y); g.stroke(); }
    g.fillStyle = '#fff';
    for (const b of [-60, -30, -20, -10, 10, 20, 30, 60]) gTick(g, 0, 0, b * DEG, r - 2 * f, (Math.abs(b) % 30 ? 5 : 9) * f, 2 * f);
    g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(0, -r + 2 * f); g.lineTo(-5 * f, -r + 11 * f); g.lineTo(5 * f, -r + 11 * f); g.fill();
    g.restore();
    g.fillStyle = '#f2a33a'; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(cx, cy - r + 13 * f); g.lineTo(cx - 5 * f, cy - r + 21 * f); g.lineTo(cx + 5 * f, cy - r + 21 * f); g.fill();
    g.strokeStyle = '#f2a33a'; g.lineWidth = 3 * f; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(cx - 34 * f, cy); g.lineTo(cx - 12 * f, cy); g.lineTo(cx - 6 * f, cy + 6 * f); g.moveTo(cx + 34 * f, cy); g.lineTo(cx + 12 * f, cy); g.lineTo(cx + 6 * f, cy + 6 * f); g.stroke();
    g.fillStyle = '#f2a33a'; g.beginPath(); g.arc(cx, cy, 2.5 * f, 0, 7); g.fill();
  },
  alt(g, cx, cy, r, v) {
    const f = r / 62, alt = v.alt;
    gaugeBezel(g, cx, cy, r, 'ALT  ×100 ft');
    g.fillStyle = '#eee'; g.font = `700 ${Math.round(12 * f)}px ${MONO}`; g.textAlign = 'center'; g.textBaseline = 'middle';
    for (let i = 0; i < 50; i++) { const an = i / 50 * 2 * Math.PI; gTick(g, cx, cy, an, r - 2 * f, (i % 5 ? 5 : 10) * f, 1.2 * f); if (i % 5 === 0) g.fillText(i / 5, cx + Math.sin(an) * (r - 19 * f), cy - Math.cos(an) * (r - 19 * f)); }
    rr(g, cx + 14 * f, cy - 8 * f, 34 * f, 16 * f, 2); g.fillStyle = '#222'; g.fill(); g.fillStyle = '#ddd'; g.font = `600 ${Math.round(10 * f)}px ${MONO}`; g.fillText('29.92', cx + 31 * f, cy);
    needle(g, cx, cy, (alt % 10000) / 10000 * 2 * Math.PI, r * 0.5, 4.5 * f);
    needle(g, cx, cy, (alt % 1000) / 1000 * 2 * Math.PI, r - 8 * f, 2.6 * f);
    g.fillStyle = '#fff'; gTick(g, cx, cy, (alt % 100000) / 100000 * 2 * Math.PI, r - 1, 12 * f, 2 * f);
  },
  tc(g, cx, cy, r, v) {
    const f = r / 62, tilt = clamp(v.rate / 3, -2, 2) * 20 * DEG;
    gaugeBezel(g, cx, cy, r, '2 MIN TURN');
    g.fillStyle = '#eee'; g.font = `700 ${Math.round(11 * f)}px ${UI}`; g.textAlign = 'center'; g.textBaseline = 'middle';
    for (const a of [-20, 0, 20]) { g.save(); g.translate(cx, cy); g.rotate(a * DEG); g.fillRect(-r + 8 * f, -1.5 * f, 14 * f, 3 * f); g.fillRect(r - 22 * f, -1.5 * f, 14 * f, 3 * f); g.restore(); }
    g.fillText('L', cx - r + 14 * f, cy + 22 * f); g.fillText('R', cx + r - 14 * f, cy + 22 * f);
    g.save(); g.translate(cx, cy - 4 * f); g.rotate(tilt); g.fillStyle = '#f4f4f4';
    g.fillRect(-r + 14 * f, -2 * f, (r - 14 * f) * 2, 4 * f); g.beginPath(); g.arc(0, 0, 6 * f, 0, 7); g.fill(); g.fillRect(-1.5 * f, -12 * f, 3 * f, 10 * f); g.restore();
    const bx = cx, by = cy + 30 * f; rr(g, bx - 34 * f, by - 8 * f, 68 * f, 16 * f, 8 * f); g.fillStyle = '#d9d4b8'; g.fill();
    g.strokeStyle = '#111'; g.lineWidth = 1.5 * f; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(bx - 9 * f, by - 8 * f); g.lineTo(bx - 9 * f, by + 8 * f); g.moveTo(bx + 9 * f, by - 8 * f); g.lineTo(bx + 9 * f, by + 8 * f); g.stroke();
    g.fillStyle = '#111'; g.beginPath(); g.arc(bx + clamp(v.slip, -26, 26) * f, by, 6.5 * f, 0, 7); g.fill();
  },
  hi(g, cx, cy, r, v) {
    const f = r / 62;
    gaugeBezel(g, cx, cy, r);
    g.save(); g.translate(cx, cy); g.rotate(-v.hdg * DEG);
    g.fillStyle = '#eee'; g.font = `700 ${Math.round(11 * f)}px ${MONO}`; g.textAlign = 'center'; g.textBaseline = 'middle';
    for (let d = 0; d < 360; d += 5) { g.save(); g.rotate(d * DEG); g.fillRect(-0.6 * f, -r + 2 * f, 1.2 * f, (d % 10 ? 4 : 8) * f); if (d % 30 === 0) g.fillText(d === 0 ? 'N' : d === 90 ? 'E' : d === 180 ? 'S' : d === 270 ? 'W' : d / 10, 0, -r + 19 * f); g.restore(); }
    g.restore();
    g.fillStyle = '#f2a33a'; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(cx, cy - 24 * f); g.lineTo(cx - 12 * f, cy + 16 * f); g.lineTo(cx, cy + 10 * f); g.lineTo(cx + 12 * f, cy + 16 * f); g.closePath(); g.fill();
    g.fillRect(cx - f, cy - r + 2 * f, 2 * f, 12 * f);
  },
  vsi(g, cx, cy, r, v) {
    const f = r / 62;
    gaugeBezel(g, cx, cy, r, 'VERT SPEED ×100');
    g.fillStyle = '#eee'; g.font = `700 ${Math.round(11 * f)}px ${MONO}`; g.textAlign = 'center'; g.textBaseline = 'middle';
    for (let k = -20; k <= 20; k += 5) { const an = vsiAng(k * 100); gTick(g, cx, cy, an, r - 2 * f, (k % 10 ? 6 : 10) * f, 1.4 * f); if (k % 10 === 0) g.fillText(Math.abs(k), cx + Math.sin(an) * (r - 20 * f), cy - Math.cos(an) * (r - 20 * f)); }
    g.fillText('UP', cx - 18 * f, cy - 22 * f); g.fillText('DN', cx - 18 * f, cy + 22 * f);
    needle(g, cx, cy, vsiAng(v.vs), r - 6 * f, 3 * f);
  },
  tach(g, cx, cy, r, v) {
    const f = r / 62;
    gaugeBezel(g, cx, cy, r, 'RPM ×100');
    g.strokeStyle = '#2fbf4f'; g.lineWidth = 5 * f; g.beginPath(); g.arc(cx, cy, r - 4 * f, tachAng(2100) - Math.PI / 2, tachAng(2700) - Math.PI / 2); g.stroke();
    g.fillStyle = '#eee'; g.font = `700 ${Math.round(11 * f)}px ${MONO}`; g.textAlign = 'center'; g.textBaseline = 'middle';
    for (let k = 0; k <= 3500; k += 500) { const an = tachAng(k); gTick(g, cx, cy, an, r - 2 * f, 9 * f, 1.4 * f); if (k % 1000 === 0) g.fillText(k / 100, cx + Math.sin(an) * (r - 20 * f), cy - Math.cos(an) * (r - 20 * f)); }
    needle(g, cx, cy, tachAng(v.rpm), r - 8 * f, 3 * f);
    g.fillStyle = v.dead ? '#e03a3e' : '#9aa4ad'; g.font = `700 ${Math.round(10 * f)}px ${UI}`; g.fillText(v.dead ? 'ENGINE OUT' : `THR ${Math.round(v.thr * 100)}%`, cx, cy + 34 * f);
  },
  nav(g, cx, cy, r, v) {
    const f = r / 62, N = v.nav, ils = N.type === 'ILS';
    gaugeBezel(g, cx, cy, r);
    g.save(); g.translate(cx, cy); g.rotate(-N.obs * DEG);
    g.fillStyle = '#ddd'; g.font = `700 ${Math.round(10 * f)}px ${MONO}`; g.textAlign = 'center'; g.textBaseline = 'middle';
    for (let d = 0; d < 360; d += 10) { g.save(); g.rotate(d * DEG); g.fillRect(-0.5 * f, -r + 2 * f, f, (d % 30 ? 4 : 7) * f); if (d % 30 === 0) g.fillText(d / 10, 0, -r + 16 * f); g.restore(); }
    g.restore();
    g.fillStyle = '#f2a33a'; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(cx, cy - r + f); g.lineTo(cx - 5 * f, cy - r + 9 * f); g.lineTo(cx + 5 * f, cy - r + 9 * f); g.fill();
    g.fillStyle = '#ddd'; for (let k = -5; k <= 5; k++) if (k) { g.beginPath(); g.arc(cx + k * 7 * f, cy, 1.8 * f, 0, 7); g.fill(); }
    const on = N.flag !== 'OFF';
    g.strokeStyle = '#f4f4f4'; g.lineWidth = 3 * f; const dx = (on ? clamp(N.cdi, -5, 5) : 0) * 7 * f; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(cx + dx, cy - 38 * f); g.lineTo(cx + dx, cy + 38 * f); g.stroke();
    if (ils) { for (let k = -2; k <= 2; k++) if (k) { g.beginPath(); g.arc(cx, cy + k * 12 * f, 1.8 * f, 0, 7); g.fill(); }
      const dy = (on && N.gsValid !== false ? clamp(N.gs, -2.5, 2.5) : 0) * 12 * f; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(cx - 36 * f, cy + dy); g.lineTo(cx + 36 * f, cy + dy); g.stroke(); }
    g.font = `700 ${Math.round(10 * f)}px ${UI}`; g.fillStyle = on ? '#2fbf4f' : '#e03a3e';
    g.fillText(ils ? (on ? 'ILS' : 'LOC OFF') : N.flag, cx + 28 * f, cy - 22 * f);
    g.fillStyle = '#9aa4ad'; g.fillText(ils ? `LOC ${Math.round(N.obs)}°` : `OBS ${String(Math.round(N.obs)).padStart(3, '0')}`, cx, cy + 50 * f);
    g.fillText(`DME ${(N.dme || 0).toFixed(1)}`, cx - 28 * f, cy - 22 * f);
  },
  compass(g, cx, cy, r, v) {          // r = half width of the strip
    const W = r * 2, H = r * 0.24, x = cx - r, y = cy - H / 2, k = W / 75;
    rr(g, x, y, W, H, 4); g.fillStyle = '#0a0c0f'; g.fill();
    g.save(); g.beginPath(); g.rect(x, y, W, H); g.clip();
    g.fillStyle = '#e8e8e8'; g.font = `700 ${Math.round(H * 0.45)}px ${MONO}`; g.textAlign = 'center'; g.textBaseline = 'middle';
    for (let d = -60; d <= 60; d += 5) { const hh = Math.round(v.chdg / 5) * 5 + d, xx = cx + (hh - v.chdg) * k, n = ((hh % 360) + 360) % 360;
      if (n % 30 === 0) g.fillText(n === 0 ? 'N' : n === 90 ? 'E' : n === 180 ? 'S' : n === 270 ? 'W' : String(n / 10), xx, cy - H * 0.05); else g.fillRect(xx - 0.5, y + H * 0.72, 1, H * 0.28); }
    g.restore(); g.fillStyle = '#ff8a3a'; g.fillRect(cx - 1, y, 2, H);
  },
};
// current aircraft values, including the effects of failures
function sixVals(att) {
  const s = sim, fails = SCHOOL.fail, vac = fails.vacuum ? clamp((R.time - fails.vacuum) / 25, 0, 1) : 0;
  const N = navDeviation(), rate = SCHOOL.active ? SCHOOL.rateF || 0 : -qrot(s.q, s.w)[1] / DEG;
  return {
    kias: s.ias * KTS, alt: s.pos[1] * FT, vs: s.vs * 196.85, rate, slip: clamp(-s.beta / DEG * 2.2, -26, 26),
    pitch: lerp(att.pitch, 6, vac), bank: lerp(att.bank, 18 + 10 * Math.sin(R.time / 7), vac), hdg: att.hdg + vac * (R.time - (fails.vacuum || 0)) * 1.4,
    chdg: att.hdg + Math.sin(R.time * 1.3) * Math.min(3, Math.abs(rate) * 1.5),
    rpm: s.dead ? Math.min(s.rpm, 900) : s.rpm, thr: s.thr, dead: !!s.dead,
    nav: Object.assign({}, N, { obs: N.type === 'ILS' ? RWY.hdg : SCHOOL.obs }),
  };
}
// plain-language readings shown under each gauge (K key)
const LR = v => v > 0 ? '右' : '左';
function gaugeCaption(k, v) {
  const V = ACFT.v;
  switch (k) {
    case 'asi': { const z = v.kias < 30 ? '低速（地上・停止中）' : v.kias < V.s0 ? '失速域' : v.kias > V.ne ? '赤線超え！' : v.kias > V.no ? '黄＝注意' : v.kias <= V.fe && v.kias >= V.s0 ? (v.kias >= V.s1 ? '緑・白＝通常／フラップ可' : '白＝フラップで飛べる') : '緑＝通常'; return [`速度 ${Math.round(v.kias)} kt`, z]; }
    case 'ai': { const p = Math.abs(v.pitch) < 1.5 ? '機首 水平' : `機首${v.pitch > 0 ? '上げ' : '下げ'} ${Math.abs(Math.round(v.pitch))}°`; const b = Math.abs(v.bank) < 2 ? '翼 水平' : `${LR(v.bank)}バンク ${Math.abs(Math.round(v.bank))}°`; return [p, b]; }
    case 'alt': { const a = Math.round(v.alt / 10) * 10; return [`高度 ${a.toLocaleString()} ft`, `短針 ${Math.floor(a / 1000) % 10}・長針 ${Math.floor(a / 100) % 10}`]; }
    case 'tc': { const r = Math.abs(v.rate), t = r < 0.6 ? 'まっすぐ' : `${LR(v.rate)}旋回 ${r.toFixed(1)}°/秒${Math.abs(r - 3) < 0.6 ? '（標準）' : ''}`; const b = Math.abs(v.slip) < 5 ? 'ボール中央：OK' : `ボール${LR(v.slip)}：${LR(v.slip)}ラダー`; return [t, b]; }
    case 'hi': return [`針路 ${String(Math.round(((v.hdg % 360) + 360) % 360)).padStart(3, '0')}°`, '上の印の下の数字'];
    case 'vsi': { const s = Math.round(v.vs / 50) * 50; return [Math.abs(s) < 100 ? '水平（昇降 0）' : `${s > 0 ? '上昇' : '降下'} ${Math.abs(s)} fpm`, '（1 分あたりの ft）']; }
    case 'tach': return [`${Math.round(v.rpm / 10) * 10} rpm`, v.dead ? 'エンジン停止' : `スロットル ${Math.round(v.thr * 100)}%`];
    case 'nav': { const N = v.nav; if (N.flag === 'OFF') return [N.type === 'ILS' ? 'ILS 圏外' : 'VOR 局上空', '針は無効']; const c = Math.abs(N.cdi) < 0.5 ? 'コース上' : `針${LR(N.cdi)}→${LR(N.cdi)}へ`;
      if (N.type === 'ILS') { const gsT = N.gsValid === false ? '' : Math.abs(N.gs) < 0.4 ? '・パス上' : N.gs > 0 ? '・高い（下げる）' : '・低い（浅く）'; return [`LOC ${c}`, `GS${gsT || ' ―'}`]; }
      return [`${N.flag}・OBS ${String(Math.round(N.obs)).padStart(3, '0')}`, c]; }
  }
  return ['', ''];
}
const SIX_LAYOUT = [['asi', 0, 0], ['ai', 1, 0], ['alt', 2, 0], ['tach', 3, 0], ['tc', 0, 1], ['hi', 1, 1], ['vsi', 2, 1], ['nav', 3, 1]];
function sixPackSize() { const P = SCHOOL.explain ? 168 : 150; return [P * 4 + 20, P * 2 + 34 + (SCHOOL.explain ? 16 : 0)]; }
function drawSixPack(g, x0, y0, sc, att) {
  const v = sixVals(att), P = SCHOOL.explain ? 168 : 150, r = 62, [PW, PH] = sixPackSize();
  g.save(); g.translate(x0, y0); g.scale(sc, sc);
  rr(g, 0, 0, PW, PH, 10); g.fillStyle = 'rgba(38,42,48,0.94)'; g.fill(); g.strokeStyle = '#555c66'; g.lineWidth = 1.5; g.stroke();
  GAUGES.compass(g, 10 + P * 1.5 + P / 2, 15, 90, v);
  g.fillStyle = '#9aa4ad'; g.font = `600 9px ${UI}`; g.textAlign = 'left'; g.textBaseline = 'middle'; g.fillText('磁気コンパス', 10 + P * 2 + 100, 15);
  for (const [k, i, j] of SIX_LAYOUT) {
    const cx = 10 + P * i + P / 2, cy = 30 + P * j + (SCHOOL.explain ? 72 : P / 2);
    GAUGES[k](g, cx, cy, r, v);
    if (SCHOOL.explain) {
      const [a, b] = gaugeCaption(k, v);
      rr(g, cx - P / 2 + 6, cy + r + 6, P - 12, 28, 4); g.fillStyle = 'rgba(6,12,22,0.9)'; g.fill();
      g.textAlign = 'center'; g.fillStyle = '#f4f4f4'; g.font = `700 11px ${UI}`; g.fillText(a, cx, cy + r + 14);
      g.fillStyle = '#8fd8ff'; g.font = `600 9.5px ${UI}`; g.fillText(b, cx, cy + r + 27);
    }
  }
  const s = sim;
  g.fillStyle = '#c9ced6'; g.font = `600 10px ${UI}`; g.textAlign = 'left';
  g.fillText(`FLAPS ${flapName(s.flapIdx)}  TRIM ${(s.trim >= 0 ? '▲' : '▼') + Math.abs(Math.round(s.trim * 100))}   I：計器切替  K：読み方の表示 ${SCHOOL.explain ? 'ON' : 'OFF'}  [ ]：OBS`, 14, PH - 8);
  g.restore();
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
//  instrument tutorial: one gauge per page, animated, with labelled parts
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
const V_ = () => ACFT.v;
const baseVals = () => ({ kias: 95, alt: 3000, vs: 0, rate: 0, slip: 0, pitch: 0, bank: 0, hdg: 90, chdg: 90, rpm: 2400, thr: 0.7, dead: false, nav: { type: 'VOR', obs: 90, cdi: 0, gs: 0, flag: 'TO', dme: 12.4, gsValid: true } });
const INST_PAGES = [
  { t: '6 つの計器（ベーシック T）', g: 'panel', vals: t => Object.assign(baseVals(), { kias: 95, alt: 3450 + Math.sin(t / 3) * 60, vs: Math.sin(t / 3) * 400, bank: Math.sin(t / 4) * 15, pitch: 2, rate: Math.sin(t / 4) * 2, hdg: 90 + t * 2 }),
    p: ['アナログの練習機では、この 6 つが<b>基本の 6 計器</b>。上の段が「速度・姿勢・高度」、下の段が「旋回・方位・昇降」です。', '中央上の<b>姿勢指示器</b>がいちばん大事。そこから他の計器へ目を往復させます（クロスチェック）。', '右の列は<b>回転計（エンジン）</b>と<b>航法計器（VOR/ILS）</b>。上の細い帯は<b>磁気コンパス</b>。', '次のページから 1 つずつ見ていきます。飛行中は <kbd>K</kbd> で各計器の下に「いまの読み」が文字で出ます。'] },
  { t: '① 速度計（対気速度計）', g: 'asi', vals: t => Object.assign(baseVals(), { kias: 70 + Math.sin(t / 2) * 60 }),
    notes: [[0, -0.75, 0.95, -1.05, '針：いまの速度（kt）'], [-0.6, 0.55, -1.3, 0.8, '白の帯：フラップを出せる範囲'], [0.35, -0.8, 1.25, -0.35, '緑の帯：ふつうに使う範囲'], [0.78, 0.35, 1.28, 0.5, '黄の帯：荒れた空気では禁止'], [0.62, 0.72, 0.9, 1.08, '赤線：絶対に超えない速度']],
    p: [() => `針が指す数字がいまの速度。単位は <b>kt（ノット）</b>、1 kt ≒ 時速 1.85 km。`, () => `<b>白の帯</b>（${V_().s0}〜${V_().fe} kt）：フラップを出してよい範囲。下の端 ${V_().s0} kt がフラップ全開の失速速度（Vs0）。`, () => `<b>緑の帯</b>（${V_().s1}〜${V_().no} kt）：通常の範囲。下の端 ${V_().s1} kt がフラップなしの失速速度（Vs1）。`, () => `<b>黄色の帯</b>（${V_().no}〜${V_().ne} kt）：穏やかな空気でのみ。<b>赤い線</b> ${V_().ne} kt（Vne）は絶対に超えない。`, 'この速度計は「空気に対する速さ」。向かい風だと地面に対しては遅く進みます。'] },
  { t: '② 姿勢指示器', g: 'ai', vals: t => Object.assign(baseVals(), { pitch: Math.sin(t / 2.2) * 12, bank: Math.sin(t / 3.1) * 35 }),
    notes: [[0, 0.02, -1.35, 0.35, 'オレンジの記号＝自分の機体（動かない）'], [0, -0.55, -1.3, -0.75, '青＝空'], [0, 0.6, 1.3, 0.8, '茶色＝地面'], [0.28, -0.88, 1.2, -1.05, '上の目盛り：バンク角 10・20・30・60°'], [0.3, -0.35, 1.3, -0.3, '横線：機首の上下 10° ごと']],
